#2532. Caïazzo derrrière Diallo

Sur Eurosport, Bernard Caïzzo affiche son soutien à Philippe Diallo, candidat à la présidence de la FIFA. Extraits.

"C'est courageux de sa part, parce qu'il n'y a pas beaucoup de gens qui voulaient y aller. Là, c'est un peu aller à la guerre contre Infantino, avec tout ce que ça représente, ce n'est pas simple. Je l'ai eu comme collaborateur au niveau du syndicat des clubs et c'est quelqu'un d'assez calme, humble, qui connaît ses dossiers et sait écouter les gens. Et il ne cherche pas les honneurs, pas du tout. Philippe Diallo est plus dans le travail. Et s'il peut, au niveau du football mondial, apporter une plus grande intégrité, parce que c'est le mal dont on souffre, je m'en félicite. C'est avant tout un manager, il a bien géré tout ce qu'il a eu à gérer dans sa vie. Moi, je trouve que sa candidature est vraiment une très, très bonne nouvelle.

C'est quelqu'un qui, par sa personnalité, par son calme dans les réunions, par le fait qu'il a une grande capacité d'écoute, parce qu'il connaît très bien les dossiers, a souvent un avis pertinent. Moi, je sais que quand je travaillais avec lui, souvent, quand on se parlait, j'allais le voir dans son bureau et il était très clair. Il voyait très bien les choses à l'avance. Je pense que ses origines sénégalaises l'aideront à se rapprocher du continent africain. Philippe peut gagner. Il n'a pas de casserole parce qu'il est intègre, parce qu'il est droit. Il n'est pas du tout dans la compromission, dans l'argent. C'est un atout de poids.  C'est l'anti-Infantino. Et je trouve que c'est une candidature rafraîchissante."

#2530. Frattesi si allena con la Lazio dopo gli esami: può esserci col Lecce

La risonanza ha evidenziato un affaticamento, senza lesioni. Rovella e Gudmundsson hanno lavorato con il gruppo.

Davide Frattesi è tornato ad allenarsi regolarmente con il gruppo della Lazio dopo il problema ai flessori che lo aveva costretto a lasciare in anticipo il ritiro della Nazionale. Gli esami hanno escluso lesioni muscolari e, salvo imprevisti nelle prossime sedute, il centrocampista sarà a disposizione di Gennaro Gattuso per la gara contro il Lecce.

CLICCA QUI E SEGUI LAZIOPRESS.IT ANCHE SU INSTAGRAM

Il classe ’99, ex Inter, aveva lasciato Coverciano nei giorni scorsi per un fastidio accusato durante gli impegni con l’Italia. Aveva così saltato la sfida di Bruxelles contro il Belgio, mentre alla Lazio cresceva la preoccupazione per le sue condizioni.

Gli accertamenti strumentali e la risonanza magnetica effettuati nelle ultime ore hanno evidenziato un affaticamento, senza lesioni muscolari. Nella seduta odierna Frattesi ha lavorato con i compagni, mettendosi a disposizione dello staff tecnico dopo il rientro anticipato dal ritiro azzurro.

Se non arriveranno ricadute o altri imprevisti durante la preparazione, il centrocampista sarà convocabile per l’anticipo della 12ª giornata di Serie A contro il Lecce. La partita è in programma sabato 21 novembre alle 15:00 allo Stadio Olimpico.

Il recupero di Frattesi conta per la Lazio anche alla luce del suo rendimento recente. La mezzala è andata a segno nell’ultimo turno di campionato sul campo del Napoli. I suoi inserimenti e la sua freschezza atletica sono risorse per affrontare la difesa del Lecce alla ripresa del torneo.

La seduta ha dato a Gattuso altre indicazioni in vista della sfida con i salentini. Nicolò Rovella, Danilo Cataldi, Albert Gudmundsson, Tijjani Noslin e Kenneth Taylor hanno lavorato regolarmente in gruppo. Il loro coinvolgimento offre al tecnico più soluzioni a centrocampo e nel reparto offensivo.

In particolare, la presenza di Rovella e Gudmundsson amplia le possibilità di rotazione dell’allenatore in vista dei prossimi impegni ravvicinati. Anche per loro, dunque, l’allenamento con i compagni è un passaggio nella preparazione della gara contro il Lecce.

#2527. Les Verts ont le moins de trous d'air

Ancien défenseur des Verts, le consultant de beIN Sports Patrice Ferri (63 ans) s'est confié à L'Union avant le déplacement de l'ASSE à Reims programmé après-demain soir. Extraits.

"Sainté, c'est la seule véritable équipe à avoir le moins de trous d'air possible et qui parvient à tenir la distance. C'est ce qu'il faut prendre en compte. Et il y a quelque chose de supplémentaire à Saint-Etienne, c'est l'engouement du public. Comme si l'équipe jouait le haut du tableau de Ligue 1. C’est un élément qui peut faire la différence, bien sûr. Mais ces clubs-là, qui ont une grosse pression, ça peut aussi les perturber à un moment donné. Ceux qui y ont été formés comme ça a été mon cas, ils connaissent très vite cet univers. Tout va plus vite dans ce club. On est tout de suite plongé dans ce milieu professionnel où les résultats sont attendus, où l'engagement de l'équipe est scruté à chaque instant. C'est comme une pieuvre.

A Saint-Etienne, la pression fait partie du club, du package. C'est comme si tu signais au PSG actuellement. C'est la même situation. Le Stade de Reims et l’ASSE ne sont pas totalement dans les mêmes dispositions en termes d'attente. Je ne suis pas persuadé que c'était tout de suite l'idée impérative du Stade de Reims de remonter. Ce n'est pas une obligation ni une nécessité, que ce soit économique ou sportive. Ils ne sont pas dans la même configuration que Saint-Etienne. Quand tu as 35 000 spectateurs au stade en Ligue 2, on se doute bien que le club ne peut pas rester des années dans la division.

Le match de ce samedi, ça fait partie des grosses rencontres. Tout le monde trouverait normal que ce soit un match de Ligue 1. Saint-Etienne a besoin de continuer à creuser l'écart qui est loin d'être énorme. De son côté, Reims a la nécessité de faire bonne figure et de réaliser un gros match. Ne serait-ce que par rapport à l'affiche, mais surtout pour ne pas se faire décrocher du top 5. Pour moi, à part Saint-Etienne, pour les autres, ce serait une erreur de vouloir uniquement se positionner pour le top 2. Les matchs sont tellement compliqués que c'est difficile de faire des séries. C'est devenu trop difficile à maîtriser."

#2525. PSV back on top of Eredivisie as Feyenoord slump in Heerenveen

See more DutchNews articles in your Google search results

See more DutchNews articles in your Google search results

PSV returned to the top of the Eredivisie with a thumping 5-2 win over Cambuur Leeuwarden as previous leaders Feyenoord lost a seven-goal thriller against the other Frisian club, Heerenveen.

It took just 46 seconds for PSV to get off the mark as Sven Mijnans headed in unmarked.

The midfielder added a second after 15 minutes and completed his hat-trick with a free-kick in the 80th minute to make the score 4-2, just as Cambuur were threatening a comeback.

Feyenoord’s dip in form continued as they let a two-goal lead slip at Heerenveen. The Rotterdammers looked to be in control going into the break with a trademark drilled shot by Anis Hadj Moussa and a penalty by Nacho Ferri.

But Maas Willemsen bundled in the ball from a corner on the stroke of half-time, before Ringo Meerveld came on for the second half and scored a hat-trick as Feyenoord’s defence crumbled under pressure.

“In the matches when we played well I saw a team that fought for each other,” Feyenoord coach Giovanni van Bronckhorst said after the game. “Mentally strong, kept running even when we didn’t have the ball. That’s our strength. When we don’t do that we’re a lesser team.”

Mexx Meerdink scored a hotly contested late penalty to settle a tempestuous game in Alkmaar as AZ came back from 3-1 down against Groningen to claim their first win in five matches.

Goalkeeper Etienne Vaessen was given a second yellow card for protesting too hard against the decision to penalise him for upending Ayoub Oufkir. Once the commotion died down Meerdink, who had missed from the spot against Vaessen earlier in the second half, made no mistake against substitute goalkeeper Lovro Stubljar.

Ajax missed the chance to take over top spot at Twente as their former striker Wout Weghorst scored a last-minute penalty to rescue a point for the home side.

Ajax had been 2-0 up with a bullet header by their giant striker Tolu Arokodare and a long-range strike by Rayane Bounida, before an own goal by Dies Janse brought the visitors back into the game.

Fortuna Sittard also had to settle for a point against Telstar after going 2-0 up thanks to a bizarre own goal by Sem Valk. The defender’s sliced clearance looped over the head of Ronald Koeman Junior as the goalkeeper tried to head it away rather than risk being penalised for handling a backpass.

Goals just before half-time by Abdelnour Soualhia and Patrick Brouwer brought Telstar back into the game, but neither side was able to find a winner after the break.

That result allowed ADO, under the guidance of interim coach Levi Schwiebbe, to close the gap on 16th-placed Telstar to one point with a 3-1 win against Sparta, while bottom Willem II collected their third point of the season at home to PEC Zwolle.

#2522. Pas de Champagne pour les supporters des Verts

On s'y attendait un peu. C'est désormais officiel : Il n'y aura pas de supporters de l'AS Saint-Étienne au stade Auguste-Delaune ce samedi 21 novembre. En tous cas, pas officiellement. En vue du choc de la 13ème journée du championnat de Ligue 2 face au Stade de Reims, les fans de l'ASSE ont effectivement été interdits de déplacement dans la Marne. Dans son arrêté, comme d'habitude, la préfecture locale s'appuie notamment sur des incidents survenus ces dernières saisons entre supporters rémois et stéphanois pour justifier cette interdiction de déplacement. Il est ainsi "interdit sur le territoire de la ville de Reims à toute personne se prévalant de la qualité de supporter de l'ASSE ou se comportant comme tel de circuler ou de stationner sur la voie."La décision de la préfecture de la Marne a été consolidée par un arrêté du ministère de l'Intérieur. Le parcage stéphanois restera donc vide pour ce duel entre deux prétendants à la montée en Ligue 1.

#2521. Brilliant Netherlands Put Seven past Greece

Football-Oranje's Michael and Abdul review round 11 of the Eredivisie ...

Football-Oranje's Michael and Abdul review round 10 of the Eredivisie ...

Football-Oranje's Michael and Abdul review round 9 of the Eredivisie ...

Football-Oranje's Michael and Abdul review round 8 of the Eredivisie ...

Football-Oranje's Michael and Abdul review round 6 of the Eredivisie ...

Football-Oranje's Michael and Abdul review round 5 of the Eredivisie ...

Is AZ Alkmaar's academy now the best in the Netherlands? ...

Football-Oranje's Michael and Abdul review round 4 of the Eredivisie ...

It's time for our BIG Dutch Eredivisie season preview! Football-Oranje's ...

A new season of Dutch football is here! Football-Oranje's Michael ...

Football-Oranje's Michael and Abdul react to the Netherlands' opening Nations ...

Football-Oranje's Michael and Abdul react to the Netherlands' Nations League ...

The Netherlands were in excellent form on Friday as they hammered Greece 7-0 to all but seal their spot in the Nations League Quarter-finals.

Xavi had Frenkie de Jong back in the midfield as he partnered Joey Veerman in midfield. Jurrien Timber played left-back as Micky van de Ven replaced the missing Virgil van Dijk. Up front, Donyell Malen led the line.

The first half was a joy to behold for Oranje fans as Netherlands ran rampant with five goals. Crysencio Summerville got the party started with a fine finish in the sixth minute. Veerman then sent an excellent through ball for Tijjani Reijnders to make it 2-0.

The Netherlands were playing lovely football with Veerman, Timber and Malen all involved. It was quickly 3-0 as Reijnders tapped in before the midfielder completed his hattrick in the 33rd minute. Before the break, Timber added the fifth.

In the second half, Teun Koopmeiners replaced De Jong but the Netherlands kept pushing forward and Timber got his second to make it 6-0. Malen then got amongst the goals for the seventh on the hour mark.

Xavi then rang more changes with Ruben van Bommel, Noa Lang, Gjivai Zechiel and Ian Maatsen all coming on. The changes took away some of the momentum and there was no further goals before the end.

The Netherlands are now almost guaranteed a place in the quarter-finals and only a huge loss to Germany on Monday would prevent this.

A wonderful evening for the Netherlands.7-0 winners over Greece!Reijnders, Veerman, Malen, Timber and Summerville all excellent.

#2520. Un psaume pour Zoumana Camara

Suivant de près l'actualité du MHSC, Benjamin Psaume est revenu dans le podcast Montpellier Sport Club (relayé par Allez Paillade) sur le match de Coupe de France qui a vu les Héraultais écraser (5-0) le FC Balagne en Corse. L'ancien joueur de Toulouse, Nîmes, Arles-Avignon, Troyes et Le Puy ne tarit pas d'éloges sur Zoumana Camara :

"En coupe de France, dans des matchs comme cela où il manque beaucoup de monde, le plus important c’est la qualification. Zoumana Camara a lancé des jeunes. C’est à l’image de ce qu’il montre depuis le début de saison. Il a tellement fédéré le groupe et le vestiaire que peu importe les joueurs qu’il peut mettre, on sent qu’ils sont animés par une envie. Ils ne réfléchissent pas au contexte même s’il est particulier, ils ont envie de faire mal à l’adversaire. Ils ont un enthousiasme dès la première minute. Ils répondent présents. Et quand tu marques à la première minute dans des matchs comme ça, je me suis dit que c’était terminé même si après ils ont eu des occasions. Quand tu es le gros face au “petit”, tu sais que si tu ouvres le score c’est plus facile.

Le football a changé car avant, ils mettaient le bus et là tu arrives à les prendre dans la profondeur au bout d’une minute. Cela n’existait pas à mon époque quand tu allais jouer chez une équipe de niveau inférieur. Il n’y a pas eu trop de coups, le synthétique n’a pas joué de mauvais tour non plus. Il n’y a pas eu l’ambiance corse qu’il y avait avant. Tu te rends le match facile. On a découvert des jeunes joueurs. Ils ont réussi sur un tour où on ne leur en aurait pas voulu sur la manière à la mettre pour l’emporter en respectant l’adversaire. Ils ont donné une bonne image du club, ils ont également laissé la recette. À deux ou trois à zéro, ils ont continué. Ce vestiaire, ce groupe là, dans la psychologie, cela m’aurait étonné qu’ils prennent le match de haut. C’est très intéressant et à l’image de ce que l’on voit depuis le début de la saison."

#2518. Lazio, mercato di gennaio: Lotito lavora da due mesi per il saldo zero

Gli agenti hanno sondato i big: il Brentford ha chiesto Isaksen e la Juventus è pronta a offrire 40 milioni per Taylor.

Per la Lazio resta aperta la possibilità di operare nel mercato di gennaio a saldo zero, anziché subire un nuovo blocco totale. Lotito lavora da due mesi per trovare una soluzione. La decisione arriverà a dicembre dalla Commissione di controllo.

CLICCA QUI E SEGUI LAZIOPRESS.IT ANCHE SU INSTAGRAM

A settembre il blocco invernale sembrava inevitabile, dopo gli acquisti estivi e la crescita del monte ingaggi. Ora nei conti biancocelesti si intravede uno spiraglio, seppure stretto. Lotito procede senza annunci, tra trattative, incastri e verifiche dei conti; a Formello restano prudenza e speranza.

La Commissione di controllo, che ieri ha firmato un protocollo d’intesa con la Consob, darà la risposta definitiva a dicembre. Fino a quel momento, l’ipotesi del saldo zero rimane aperta, ma non è una certezza.

Nel frattempo è pronto un piano alternativo. Gli agenti hanno sondato il mercato, raccolto offerte e valutato i big della Lazio. La linea del club, però, è quella promessa a Gattuso: nessuna cessione a gennaio.

Il Brentford ha chiesto Isaksen. Anche la Juventus potrebbe scontrarsi con la stessa posizione: è pronta a mettere sul tavolo 40 milioni per Taylor. La linea sulle cessioni è riassunta così: «In uscita non si muove nessuno». Almeno per ora, in attesa della decisione prevista prima di Natale.

#2513. Les All Blacks c'est l'ambiance de Geoffroy-Guichard

À l’occasion du podcast Montpellier Sport Club, disponible en vidéo et en audio depuis ce jeudi 19 novembre sur le site du journal Midi Libre, l’entraîneur du MHSC Zoumana Camara a raconté comment il avait puisé dans ses souvenirs d’un haka des All Blacks et la réaction du XV de France pour préparer le déplacement de son équipe à Saint-Étienne.

"Je me nourris de tout." Comme de nombreux entraîneurs, Zoumana Camara aime aller puiser dans d’autres sports pour nourrir sa méthode. Amateur de basket-ball NBA, de handball ou d’athlétisme, dont il admire le fait de se "préparer pour une compétition qui dure 1’30, un saut, dix secondes", le coach du MHSC regarde également du côté du rugby international. Une source ovale au sein de laquelle il a récemment déniché une inspiration.

"Quand on a préparé Saint-Étienne et qu’on est allés jouer là-bas (le 5 septembre), je sais que j’ai une jeune équipe, qu’on va jouer à Geoffroy-Guichard où l’ambiance est un douzième homme. Alors j’ai voulu faire une causerie un peu différente. Elle m’est venue le matin (du match). Je me suis souvenu que lorsque j’étais plus jeune, j’avais aimé la France lorsqu’elle avait affronté les All Blacks, face à face au moment du haka (en 2007). Et je voulais montrer à mes joueurs que l’environnement, il faut l’affronter de face avant que le match commence et leur dire que les All Blacks, ce sera l’ambiance de Geoffroy-Guichard", détaille-t-il.

À cette comparaison s’est également ajouté un changement d’habitude. "Pour la première fois, j’ai dit au staff d’aller faire l’échauffement à côté du Kop (des Verts). Et pas en plein milieu, pour ne pas fuir", explique Zoumana Camara.

#2511. Lazio, mercato: Fabiani riattiva la talent room, Lang tra i nomi

Le priorità sono una mezz’ala e un vice Zaccagni; per l’esterno destro lo sguardo è rivolto a giugno.

Fabiani ha rimesso in moto la talent room della Lazio. Secondo Il Messaggero, gli svincolati non convincono: la ricerca riguarda giovani, occasioni e profili per i quali servono risorse. Il lavoro prosegue come se il mercato fosse ancora possibile, o per farsi trovare pronti nel caso in cui lo diventasse.

CLICCA QUI E SEGUI LAZIOPRESS.IT ANCHE SU INSTAGRAM

#2510. Johan Cruyff’s grandson called up to play for USA under-18 team

See more DutchNews articles in your Google search results

See more DutchNews articles in your Google search results

The most famous name in Dutch football could appear on an international men’s team sheet for the first time in 30 years after Johan Cruyff’s grandson was called up for the United States’ upcoming matches in Europe.

Shae Cruijff-Beane, the son of Johan Cruyff’s daughter Chantal and her American husband Todd Beane, was included in the squad that will play against Norway and Portugal in the next seven days.

Cruijff-Beane plays as a midfielder for Gimnàstic de Tarragona, a club in the Spanish third tier based near Barcelona. His older brother Jordan also came through the club’s ranks and is currently playing US college football at Westmont College in California.

Shae, 17, is eligible to play for the USA through his father and Spain and the Netherlands through his mother, who spent part of her childhood in Catalonia while Johan Cruyff was playing for Barcelona in the 1970s.

The USA under-18 squad also includes an Ajax player, midfielder Xander Newstead, who grew up in San Francisco and joined the Amsterdam club’s youth team set-up at the age of 10, beforec signing professional terms last year.

Johan Cruyff went on to play in the United States for Los Angeles Aztecs and Washington Diplomats before returning to the Netherlands, winding down his career at Ajax and Feyenoord.

His son Jordi played for Barcelona and Manchester United and won nine international caps in 1996. Last year he was appointed technical director at Ajax, the club where his father began his career.

#2508. Souvenirs, souvenirs

Le Patriote, édition Beaujolais Val de Saône, aussitôt la victoire des Verts acquise à Rumilly, signifiant un ASSE-FCVB pour le 8ème tour de la Coupe de France, est revenu 16 ans en arrière. Il fait un flash-back sur cette rencontre du 3 février 2010, qui avait vu les Verts l'emporter péniblement sur les Caladois (2-2 ap pro, 1-3 aux tab) en 16ème de finale de cette même Coupe de France.

"L'histoire qui recommence. Seize ans ont passés, depuis la dernière fête du FCVB en Coupe de France, et la venue de l'AS Saint-Etienne à Armand-Chouffet pour le compte des 16es-de-finale d'une épreuve qui avait attiré plus de 3000 personnes en Calade, comme on se donne rendez-vous pour un événement majeur dans une vie de football.Cela avait été une douleur infinie et mémorable pour le groupe du coach Jean-Michel Picollet – lui-même cœur stéphanois – mais les coéquipiers du portier si constant, Franck Laurent, s'étaient construits des souvenirs pour toute une vie. La leur. Et aujourd'hui, ce sont leurs héritiers, plus à la peine en Ligue 3, qui ont gagné le droit de défier l'ASSE, ce gros bras de la Ligue 2, dans moins de quinze jours au stade mythique de Geoffroy-Guichard."

Pour rappel, l'ASSE alignait la composition suivante : Planté - Dos Reis, Diakhité, Monsoreau, N'Daw - Matuidi, Guilavogui, Mirallas (Payet, 72ème) - Bergessio (Rivière, 94ème)., Sanogo, SakoButs : Bergessio (10ème), Sako (85ème)Tirs au but : Sanogo, Rivière, Payet (seuls 3 tirs au but avaient été nécessaires)

#2506. Lazio, Lotito e il mercato: le mosse segrete

L'accordo tecnico c'è, ma Mizuno può pareggiare l'offerta danese, superiore ai cinque milioni a stagione.

Lotito ha ottenuto una proroga per dilazionare le vecchie imposte degli anni del Covid, che pesavano per molti milioni sulla liquidità della Lazio. Secondo Il Messaggero, il presidente prova ora ad anticipare i crediti futuri degli sponsor e a inserirli nella documentazione per la Commissione, nel tentativo di trovare risorse per il mercato.

CLICCA QUI E SEGUI LAZIOPRESS.IT ANCHE SU INSTAGRAM

La dilazione delle imposte alleggerisce un peso sulla liquidità del club. Il passo successivo a cui lavora Lotito riguarda i crediti legati alle future sponsorizzazioni: anticiparli potrebbe trasformarli in risorse da presentare alla Commissione.

Il presidente ha cercato diverse soluzioni, ma non ha certezze e non vuole alimentare aspettative. Non ne ha parlato neppure con Gattuso, per evitare di illudere il tecnico.

Per Hummel il discorso è distinto da quello dei crediti degli sponsor. L'accordo tecnico c'è, ma occorre attendere che Mizuno rinunci alla prelazione che le consentirebbe di pareggiare l'offerta danese, superiore ai cinque milioni a stagione.

La situazione resta quindi legata alla scelta di Mizuno. Lotito, intanto, mantiene il silenzio anche su questo fronte.

#2505. Mykhailo Mudryk returns as Chelsea slip to Juventus defeat

Mykhailo Mudryk made his first Chelsea appearance since November 2024 as Xabi Alonso’s team succumbed to their second pre-season defeat.

Mudryk replaced Nicolas Jackson in the 82nd minute against Juventus, marking his return to competitive football after a long doping ban that was resolved this week thanks to changes in Wada regulations.

There were a couple of crosses delivered from the left flank by the Ukrainian winger, who could yet leave Chelsea on loan or on a permanent basis before the close of the transfer window.

Juventus were the victors though, thanks to a sumptuous 68th-minute goal by Kosovo winger Edon Zhegrova.

Alonso had 21 players on his bench, and made 12 second-half substitutions.

Levi Colwill and Cole Palmer featured in the defeat by 10-man Spurs on Saturday, but sat this game out as they continue to build fitness. Danny Welbeck did start though, reunited with former Brighton team-mate Joao Pedro up top.

Chelsea scored just one goal against Spurs and Juventus, but the thrust provided by right-back Marco Palestra in tandem with first Geovany Quenda and then Estevao was a bright spot for Alonso.

Chelsea continue their pre-season against AC Milan on Saturday, before they host Real Sociedad at Stamford Bridge on August 15.

They begin their Premier League campaign at Fulham in the Monday night slot on the opening weekend.

Even Chelsea’s in-house streaming had given up the ghost by the end. Consecutive pre-season defeats for Chelsea, but worth remembering that Colwill and Palmer sat out this fitness exercise and Enzo Fernandez is still to return. Palestra’s performances a real positive but the team is still finding its way.

Juventus win a corner late on, but Yildiz’s delivery is too close to Penders. Just two minutes for Chelsea to find an equaliser.

Mudryk does well to dig out a cross from the left but the goalkeeper Perin gathers. There will be four minutes of added time to play.

Chelsea not really threatening an equaliser as the game meanders towards a conclusion. Should they score a goal, we will have a penalty shoot-out.

Estevao looked for Mudryk with a reverse pass between full-back and centre-back but there was too much weight on the ball.

Estevao’s free-kick is saved but the bigger news for Chelsea fans is that Mykhailo Mudryk has been introduced from the bench, replacing Nicolas Jackson for the closing stages.

Lavia and Liam Delap involved in some handbags with the Juventus players, Bremer the centre-back in the thick of things too and has been shown a yellow card. Chelsea have a free-kick, Estevao over it from 35 yards out.

Just the one goal scored for Chelsea against top level opponents in pre-season, but I doubt Alonso will be panicking just yet. Chelsea play Milan this weekend before hosting Real Sociedad on August 15th in their final pre season friendly.

Juventus goalkeeper Perin sells Nicolas Jackson a dummy so he can pick a pass into midfield. The Italian side controlling the game quite well since the goal, against a weaker Chelsea team.

Chelsea’s younger players will have to dig them out of trouble in the last 20 minutes. There has not been much in the game, Chelsea have shaded it in general play without quite creating enough.

The Kosovan winger with a first-time, left-footed hit from almost 25 yards, curled into the top corner. Nothing Penders could do about it.

More changes from Chelsea: Anselmino, Acheampong, Satpaev, Assugo and Delap the players on, replacing Hato, Palestra, Gittens, Caicedo and Pedro. Alonso finishing the team with his younger players on the pitch.

Jonathan David slides a pass across the Chelsea six-yard box when there was a chance to shoot, but the flag was up for offside in any case.

Penders does well to palm away an inswinging cross from Zhegrova, before Estevao picks out the run of Palestra on the break with a brilliant forward pass. Chelsea corner, cleared. Now Caicedo has been booked for a cynical challenge to stop a break, much like Douglas Luiz.

Nicolas Jackson hacked down by Douglas Luiz after he turned on halfway with three or four Chelsea runners in support. Painful one for Jackson, and a yellow card for the former Aston Villa midfielder. Chelsea players not happy with that challenge in a pre-season game.

Chelsea certainly have the upper hand in the game, quick feet from Joao Pedro to win a free-kick in midfield. If either team is going to score the game’s first goal, you fancy it will be them.

This time Chelsea find Palestra with a ball over the top, but his shot at goal is at a comfortable height for the goalkeeper.

Palestra popping up in a centre-forward position again, but his shot towards goal is not struck cleanly. The right-back at the heart of Chelsea’s attacking again.

Two very different XIs out there for the second half.

Penders replaces Sanchez in goal, Sarr on for Tosin, Estevao on for Quenda and Jackson on for Welbeck.

For Juventus: Bremer, Perin, Zhegrova, Owusu and David are on.

Not much to write/phone/WhatsApp home about, so far. The combination between Quenda and Palestra down Chelsea’s right has been the best feature of their football, am sure we will see Estevao on that flank in the second half among other changes. The bumpy pitch and a hydration break has not helped the spectacle.

There will be four minutes of stoppage time, simply a result of the hydration break.

Far too much space opens up between Chelsea’s defence and midfield, with Meritti turning and carrying the ball forward. Boga tests Sanchez with a strike from the edge of the box. That was probably Juventus’s most threatening attack.

Palestra defends well as the last man up against Milik, Chelsea almost undone by Locatelli’s outside of the foot pass.

Robert Sanchez caught under a cross swung to the back post, very unconvincing goalkeeping, but Juventus could not get their shot away in the box. Nicoll-Jazuli then did well to bring the ball away for Chelsea at the edge of the box.

Chelsea have another corner, and yet again it comes from a right-sided attack. Chelsea are in ascendancy now after a slow start, but it is a very low intensity contest.

Caicedo with a couple of threatening crosses from a centre-right position, but no Chelsea player can connect with them cleanly. Gittens’s dribbling down the left causing problems for Cambiaso, and he was the one attacking Caicedo’s cross at the back post.

Play has now resumed after the drinks break. No changes from either team, those will come at half time. Chelsea have 21 players sitting on their bench, albeit a number of academy players are on tour.

Time for a hydration break, even though the game is being played under a closed roof.

Chelsea’s right side is their most dangerous, by a distance. Quenda does well to slip Palestra in on the overlap, and his low cross into the six-yard box is cleared for a corner. Juventus clear. Boga with a burst through the Chelsea midfield but the Juventus man then gets his pass wrong.

Kelly with a left-to-right switch to Cambiaso, and his cross deflects off the toe of Miretti in the middle and trickles just wide of the far post. Sanchez was scrambling but I think the goalkeeper had it covered.

Chelsea enjoying their most sustained spell of possession in Juventus’s half so far, without quite carving out a clear cut chance. Juventus defending their penalty area with a back five, but they do not have Bonucci, Chiellini and Barzagli back there anymore.

Quenda with a threatening inswinging cross from the right flank, but Welbeck could not quite rise high enough to direct his header towards the far corner. Quenda was keeping the width for Chelsea with Palestra inside the winger in the box. Quenda then slices a shot over from long range.

Welbeck and Caicedo involved in some good combination play, with Nicoll-Jazuli’s outside of the foot pass finding Gittens. Chelsea’s left-winger runs into traffic, but he has been one of the bright spots of the pre-season campaign thus far.

Having watched a couple of Chelsea’s friendlies, there is a lot to like about Palestra at right-back. Tall, long stride, lots of composure on the ball. Will be interesting to see if they can find a way for him and Reece James to start games. Chelsea as a team yet to find much rhythm in the game so far.

Tosin could well find himself down the pecking order this season among Chelsea’s raft of centre-backs, but he makes an important block in the area before Locatelli drags a shot wide from the edge of the box.

Former Bournemouth defender Lloyd Kelly is in the Juventus team. The Italian team are looking to press high in the early minutes whenever Chelsea try to play out from the back. Quenda with an early dart at the Juventus defence but he gets the pass all wrong with Palestra overlapping.

Palestra with a loose first touch but he recovers and defends well. Like in Sydney, this looks another very bumpy pitch, you can see the join marks between the pieces of turf that have been freshly laid. Coming out of these games without any injuries will be a priority for Alonso.

Juventus get the game started in their pale pink away kit.

Juventus XI: Di Gregorio, Kalulu, Gatti, Kelly, Cambiaso, Douglas Luiz, Locatelli, Miretti, Boga, Milik, Celik

Subs: Perrin, Pinsoglio, Nava, Bremmer, Rugani, Koopmeiners, Arthur Melo, Cabal, Owusu, Gil Puche, Oboavwoduo, Licina, Durmisi, Yildiz, Zhegrova, David

Welbeck starts against Juventus, but no Jordan Henderson with the group just yet. As Sam Dean writes here: “Last season, especially, provided evidence that the balance of their squad was not right, and they are now trying to change the internal dynamic by adding more leadership and experience to the team.”

How long will he stay there? Will he play his way back into Alonso’s team, be shipped out on loan or sold for a fee? Sam Dean assesses the options for player and club in this piece. Out of sight, out of mind could well be the most likely outcome for a player who has missed almost two years of football, and looked extremely raw before his ban.

Looks like a back four once again for Alonso, and Danny Welbeck makes his first Chelsea appearance. Pedro will play off Welbeck as a second striker. Geovany Quenda was signed last year from Sporting CP, but joined up with Chelsea this summer. Another wide forward in their collection.

Chelsea XI: Sanchez; Palestra, Fofana, Tosin, Hato; Caicedo, Nicoll-Jazuli; Quenda, Joao Pedro, Gittens; Welbeck

Chelsea subs: Penders, Sharman-Lowe, Curd, Slonina, Acheampong, Subuloye, Sarr, Diakite, Anselmino, Lavia, Essugo, Watson, Walsh, Kellyman, Mudryk, Estevao Willian, Kavuma-McQueen, Neto, Satpayev, Jackson, Delap

The wheels of commerce continue to turn for Chelsea in the transfer market but this afternoon Xabi Alonso’s team continue their preparations for the new season against Juventus in Hong Kong.

They have the chance to recover from the ignominy of losing to 10-man Tottenham Hotspur in Sydney on Saturday, but with 19 days until Chelsea’s Premier League opener against Fulham these matches are still a fitness building exercise. The positive from the Spurs game was Wesley Fofana, Levi Colwill, Cole Palmer completing 80 minutes – three players who endured injury problems last season.

Another new arrival is expected to follow Morgan Rogers, Marco Palesta, Maxence Lacroix, Jordan Henderson and Danny Welbeck to Stamford Bridge, with the club nearing a deal for Rayo Vallecano left-back Pep Chavarria.

It wouldn’t be Chelsea without talk of some eye-catching outgoings, with Spurs reportedly interested in Nicolas Jackson and Enzo Maresca eyeing a reunion with Pedro Neto at Manchester City.

Alonso’s team promise to be flexible in their tactical approach this season, but so far he has set up with a back four rather than the 3-4-2-1 system he used to such great effect at Bayer Leverkusen. That could change as availability increases closer to the start of the season, but Palestra’s bursts forward from right-back in tandem with Estevao have caught the eye.

Juventus finished sixth in Serie A last season under Luciano Spalletti, the former Roma and Napoli coach who usually guarantees expansive and attractive football. Turkey left-winger Kenan Yildiz was their top goalscorer last season, and it looks like they will make it through the summer without the left-winger being picked off by one of the Premier League’s elite. Yildiz scored 11 goals in all competitions, and Dusan Vlahovic was the only other player to reach double figures so scoring goals is a problem to address. Lois Openda and Randal Kolo Muani have been signed this summer to strengthen the forward line.

#2504. Philippe Terrier aux anges

Dans Le Patriote, édition Beaujolais Val de Saône, Philippe Terrier, le Président du FC Villefranche Beaujolais qui va affronter l'ASSE pour le compte du 8ème tour de la Coupe de France le samedi 28 novembre à 20 h, se dit partagé entre joie et soulagement de ne pas avoir à gérer l'organisation d'une telle affiche. Le chef de file du FCVB a résumé le sentiment du jour que beaucoup de Caladois devraient partager dans les jours à venir :

"C'est un vrai bonheur pour nous tous ! On s'attend à un match compliqué mais on ne veut pas que l'histoire s'arrête là. On va vivre quelque chose d'extraordinaire, après avoir découvert le Groupama Stadium face au PSG, en 2019, on va connaître Geoffroy-Guichard ! C'est extraordinaire pour le FCVB."

Une affiche qui convoque quelques souvenirs, en Calade. L'un des plus drôles concerne aussi Philippe Terrier, puisque c'est lors de la venue de Saint-Etienne en 2010 à Villefranche, que l'actuel président du FCVB avait sauté dans le bain de ses actuelles responsabilités pour prendre un poste que l'un de ses illustres prédécesseurs, Jean Gachon avait si bien occupé, des années durant" précise Le Patriote.

#2502. Dutch progress in Nations League despite 5-0 drubbing in Germany

See more DutchNews articles in your Google search results

See more DutchNews articles in your Google search results

The Netherlands came crashing down to earth after hammering Greece as they were outclassed and outmuscled by Germany in Berlin.

Oranje went into the final match of the Nations League group stage with high hopes of recording their first win over their old rivals in seven matches stretching back to 2019.

Instead they suffered their second worst defeat ever at the hands of Germany, but still progressed to the knockout stages thanks to their thumping 7-0 win in Amsterdam on Friday.

Coach Xavi Hernandez was forced to make one change in midfield as Jurriën Timber replaced Barcelona’s Frenkie de Jong, who pulled out with an obturator muscle strain.

Germany shaded the first half and went ahead five minutes before the break when Freiburg striker Derry Scherhant cut in from the left hand side and beat Bart Verbruggen with a curling strike into the top corner.

Scherhant had struck the post minutes earlier, while the Dutch might have taken the lead earlier when Crysencio Summerville slipped in the penalty area as he tried to control a cross by Cody Gakpo.

But in the second half the Dutch fell apart. In the 60th minute Florian Wirtz shoved Denzel Dumfries off the ball and had space to tee up a left-footed shot from the edge of the area that gave Verbruggen no chance.

Nico Schlotterbeck added a third from a free-kick five minutes later, before Wirtz drifted past Gakpo on the right side of the penalty area to score his second.

The humiliation was complete when Brajan Gruda capped a fine attacking performance by firing in a rebound.

Dumfries made no excuses afterwards for what he admitted was a “terrible performance”. “We gave the ball away too easily, left too much space and were too weak in the challenges,” he told NOS. “We can’t let that happen.”

#2499. Pinamonti e la prima da titolare in casa: Lazio-Lecce per il tris

Senza Zaccagni, Gattuso prepara Noslin sulla sinistra; Frattesi si è allenato in gruppo, ma resta il dubbio sul suo impiego.

Andrea Pinamonti giocherà per la prima volta da titolare all’Olimpico con la Lazio nella sfida di sabato contro il Lecce. Secondo quanto riportato da La Repubblica, L’assenza di Zaccagni gli darà ancora spazio al centro dell’attacco, con Noslin sulla sinistra al posto del capitano. Per il centravanti è un’occasione per cercare il terzo gol in biancoceleste.

CLICCA QUI E SEGUI LAZIOPRESS.IT ANCHE SU INSTAGRAM

Pinamonti sa di non aver ancora conquistato i tifosi laziali. La fiducia di Gattuso nei suoi confronti, racconta La Repubblica, alimenta però la sua voglia di riscatto e può aiutarlo a sbloccarsi anche dal primo minuto.

Nelle due precedenti gare iniziate da titolare, a Udine e Napoli, il numero 9 arrivato in estate non ha convinto. È stato sostituito nella ripresa, rispettivamente al 52’ e al 61’. Sabato spera che la prima partenza dall’inizio in casa segni un cambiamento.

Proprio all’Olimpico, entrando nel secondo tempo, ha segnato i suoi due gol con la Lazio. Il colpo di testa contro il Cagliari è stato decisivo; il piattone del 3-1 contro il Monza ha chiuso una partita rimasta fino a quel momento in bilico. Ora Pinamonti va a caccia del tris.

Contro il Lecce, Noslin prenderà il posto di Zaccagni sulla fascia sinistra, mentre Pinamonti agirà al centro. L’intesa tra l’attaccante italiano e l’olandese è ancora da affinare, ma Gattuso è convinto che la formula possa funzionare.

Il tecnico ha ricevuto segnali positivi anche da Frattesi. La mezzala ha svolto con il gruppo il primo allenamento settimanale. Il lavoro in squadra non scioglie ancora la riserva su un suo impiego dal primo minuto contro il Lecce, ma cresce l’ottimismo sulla sua presenza.

#2495. C'est un match qui donne envie

Ecrasé 6-0 lors de sa dernière confrontation avec l'ASSE, l'ancien coach palois et actuel entraîneur rémois Nicolas Usaï s'est exprimé en conférence de presse avant de recevoir les leaders stéphanois après demain-soir lors de la 13e journée de L2.

"On prépare un match de haut niveau face à certainement la meilleure équipe au vu de ce qui se passe depuis le début de saison. Notre groupe a été complété en début de semaine par des jeunes qui nous amènent toujours cet enthousiasme, cette volonté de mettre beaucoup d'intensité dans tout ce qu'ils font. On est très heureux de retrouver nos internationaux dans des conditions plutôt optimales, quand on pense par exemple à Samuel Kotto et Daouda Guindo, qui ont joué et qualifié leur pays pour la CAN. Mohamed Daramy a fait deux matchs avec le Danemark, et ce sont des matchs de très haut niveau. Au lieu de regarder le verre à moitié vide en disant qu’on n'a pas pu travailler avec eux, il faut se servir de leur expérience du très haut niveau avec beaucoup d'enthousiasme et beaucoup d'envie pour préparer ce magnifique match qui se présente samedi.

Il n’y a pas de préparation particulière par rapport à cette affiche. Juste, l'important c'est qu'on soit enthousiastes, qu'on soit audacieux dans la préparation d'un match comme ça, et qu'on ait vraiment la volonté de jouer à notre meilleur niveau, mais avec beaucoup de passion et beaucoup de plaisir. Si Saint-Etienne est l'équipe la plus complète aujourd'hui ? Oui, c'est une équipe qui est très complète et qui, dans les moments forts, est très clinique. Elle est très efficace, elle marque beaucoup de buts, elle en encaisse peu. C’est un adversaire de haut niveau. L’idée, c’est de pouvoir se confronter à un rapport de force peut-être plus élevé par rapport à ce qui se dit depuis le début de l'année, être au niveau et avoir envie de s'étalonner. On sait les ingrédients qu'on doit mettre pour faire en sorte de performer.

On sait que ça se joue aussi sur des détails. Sur ces détails-là, il va falloir être très vigilant. L'idée, c'est qu'on soit tous en éveil : le staff, les 20 joueurs qui auront le privilège d'être convoqués pour ce match-là. Mais encore une fois, le mot enthousiasme, ça va être le mot important. On va jouer chez nous, devant nos supporters. Je crois qu'il y a 16 000 ou 17 000 personnes qui sont annoncées. On doit être super heureux de jouer des affiches comme ça. Bien sûr que c’est un rendez-vous excitant à préparer. On parle beaucoup, mais l'idée, c'est d'être présent et d'être performant à l'instant T avec l'envie de poser des problèmes à cette équipe par notre capacité à maîtriser le ballon, à mettre de la vitesse, et que chaque joueur, individuellement et au service du collectif, on soit tous à notre meilleur niveau. C'est un choc entre deux places fortes du football français. C'est un match qui donne envie."

#2494. Serie A, chi rientra dopo la sosta e gli squalificati del turno

Gudmundsson è atteso contro il Lecce; Bracaglia e Ndiaye devono fermarsi per una giornata, così come il tecnico Juric.

Gudmundsson è atteso contro il Lecce e Rovella torna tra le opzioni a disposizione della Lazio. È uno dei recuperi nel quadro della Serie A aggiornato alla mattina del 18 novembre 2026, in vista della dodicesima giornata in programma dal 21 al 23 novembre. Secondo quanto riportato da DAZN, Tra gli squalificati del turno figurano Gabriele Bracaglia del Frosinone e Ndiaye del Parma; deve fermarsi per una giornata anche l’allenatore del Monza Ivan Juric.

CLICCA QUI E SEGUI LAZIOPRESS.IT ANCHE SU INSTAGRAM

La sosta ha restituito alle squadre alcuni giocatori, lasciando aperte altre valutazioni dopo gli impegni con le Nazionali. Le finestre di rientro restano indicative e le convocazioni per il prossimo turno non sono ancora definitive.

Gudmundsson ha completato il recupero ed è atteso per la partita contro il Lecce. Anche Rovella rientra tra le opzioni a disposizione.

Tra gli altri recuperi, Bellanova e Raspadori sono tornati ad allenarsi in gruppo il 17 novembre e sono attesi tra i disponibili contro l’Inter. Mina e Obert hanno svolto parte della seduta con la squadra, mentre Nzola, Aurelio e Trepy hanno ripreso il lavoro collettivo e si avvicinano al ritorno tra i convocati. Resta da confermare la disponibilità di Sugamele, già segnalato alle prese con un problema fisico.

Chalobah è atteso a disposizione contro il Cagliari. Liberali ha superato l’infortunio, ma il suo impiego è ancora da verificare. Va valutata anche la piena disponibilità di Ranieri dopo il problema al piede. Gabriele Bracaglia, invece, salterà il Milan per una giornata di squalifica.

Nell’ultimo aggiornamento sull’infermeria rossoblù, Frendrup è indicato come unico indisponibile. Venturino non compare più tra le assenze segnalate.

In casa Inter, Lautaro Martinez e Marcus Thuram si sono allenati con il gruppo il 17 novembre. Gli esami di Akanji hanno dato esito negativo dopo l’affaticamento accusato con la Svizzera; il suo rientro ad Appiano è previsto il 18 novembre. I fastidi al piede di Zielinski non destano particolare preoccupazione, ma saranno ricontrollati al ritorno dalla Nazionale.

Cambiaso, Celik e Nico Gonzalez hanno lavorato a parte anche il 17 novembre e restano in dubbio. Cambiaso è alle prese con un affaticamento alla coscia, Celik con un fastidio accusato in Nazionale; Gonzalez sta recuperando dal problema al soleo. Zhegrova ha ripreso parte del lavoro con il gruppo, mentre Conceição ha superato il problema alla spalla ed è tornato ad allenarsi con la squadra.

Berisha ha ripreso il lavoro con i compagni: il sovraccarico muscolare non lo colloca più tra le assenze certe. Per Maignan, gli esami del 17 novembre non hanno evidenziato lesioni nella regione pubo-adduttoria. Il portiere resta monitorato, ma può essere disponibile contro il Frosinone. Pavlovic ha ripreso parzialmente il lavoro in gruppo e punta alla convocazione; Terracciano è ancora da valutare dopo il trauma alla spalla.

Non sono uniformi le ricostruzioni disponibili su Ziolkowski, Pessina, Ciurria, Mout e Ngonge, presenti nelle precedenti segnalazioni. Non vengono quindi considerati assenze certe: per la loro situazione farà fede l’aggiornamento del club prima delle convocazioni. L’allenatore del Monza Ivan Juric deve scontare una giornata di squalifica.

Neres, Favasuli e Spinazzola hanno ripreso almeno parte delle sedute con la squadra e puntano alla convocazione contro il Torino. Per loro non sono più attuali le precedenti indicazioni di assenza certa fino alla fine di novembre o a dicembre.

#2493. ​Arteta’s ‘dynasty’ dream collides with Arsenal’s harsh reality

Mikel Arteta says he wants to create a “dynasty” at Arsenal as he threw down the gauntlet to his players to retain their Premier League title and reach a “different level”.

Arsenal are on the verge of completing a £75m deal for Brazil midfielder Bruno Guimãraes from Newcastle and have not given up hope of landing Real Madrid winger Vinícius Júnior, who has been offered an improved contract by the Spanish giants.

The Premier League champions slumped to a 3-1 defeat to Real Betis in Dublin on Wednesday night, when they paid the price for individual errors and a lethargic, sloppy defensive display.

But that did little to quell Arteta’s ambitions for the new season. “I’m even more excited [about this season], and we’re going to go and attack every possible title because that’s the ambition that we have,” he said.

“We know that we have the ability. We will go from a strength to a strength as well, whether it’s with the players that we have to take them to a different level, and with the market that is still open to recruit as well, so we are very optimistic about it.

“All the work that we’ve done – that I’ve done through the summer – is to understand what we need to do to win again and to create a dynasty in this football club. That’s the aim.”

Despite Arteta’s bullishness, he conceded Arsenal would have to be a lot better than they were against Betis. When asked about the mood in the dressing room, he said: “Very upset, given a hard time. They are fuming, but this is good. That’s what we need.”

Telegraph Sport analyses some of the talking points from a disappointing evening at the Aviva Stadium.

The importance of goalkeeper David Raya and centre-backs William Saliba and Gabriel Magalhães to Arsenal hardly needs overstating. Their title success was built on the foundations provided by that triumvirate at the back.

Raya has yet to report back from holiday after Spain’s World Cup triumph and Saliba is facing an extended spell on the sidelines with a back injury. Gabriel did appear for the second half but, watching this, Arteta is unlikely to have been dissuaded from the belief he needs another top defender to compensate for the loss of Saliba.

It may have been only Arsenal’s second game of pre-season, so a degree of legginess was understandable.

But the ease with which they were cut open and overloaded by the Spanish side, and the volume of individual errors, did not sit comfortably with Arteta with just over a fortnight until they kick off their title defence at home to newly promoted Coventry City.

Only once in 63 matches last season did Arsenal concede three goals in a single game but they were fortunate Betis did not score more. “In the first half there are two different things: to play well and to compete,” said Arteta, who saw his side trail 3-1 at the interval.

“We did the first one quite well in a lot of moments but the compete part – especially in and around our box – was not to the standards to win a football match against a good side. It’s better to give them [the goals away] in a friendly match than an official match. It’s always good when you lose a match in pre-season because it creates a bit more fire in the belly.”

Kepa Arrizabalaga will not want to watch Betis’s first goal back in a hurry. The Arsenal goalkeeper may have got a slight nudge as he came for the ball at a corner but he was all over the place and never looked even in the vaguest control of the situation. Rodrigo Riquelme scored at the far post after the ball sailed over the keeper. Arteta called it a “really sloppy goal”. With former Leeds goalkeeper Illan Meslier having arrived on a free transfer as back-up to Raya, Kepa faces an uncertain future at Arsenal. Moments like this will not help his cause.

He did not feature much last season but is probably best remembered for his torrid showing in the Carabao Cup final defeat by Manchester City in March. He looked as unconvincing here as he did then, as the former Chelsea goalkeeper also sliced a clearance in the build up to Betis’s third goal. Before then, Kai Havertz had turned over possession cheaply from which Nelson Deossa set up former West Ham midfielder Pablo Fornals to score.

Arsenal need more creativity and firepower in attack, hence the interest in Vinícius and Atlético Madrid’s Julián Alvarez. Viktor Gyokeres again looked rather limited here and the likes of Bukayo Saka and Eberechi Eze are also still to report back. Yet one of the few, brighter aspects of Arsenal’s performances was the sight of the regal looking Riccardo Calafiori marauding forward from a very nominal left-back role.

If Arsenal are going to play more adventurously this season and try to ratchet up things in the entertainment stakes then Calafiori could have a big role to play, as long as Arteta can keep him fit. He can be a potent weapon galloping forward and there were clear glimpses of that here. Harnessing that talent while not compromising too much defensive security may be one of the manager’s challenges.

Louie Copley had only been on the pitch for nine minutes – a half-time substitute for Ben White – when the 19-year-old midfielder went down largely unchallenged with what looked like a troubling knee injury. Arteta admitted afterwards that the way the teenager fell to the ground was a concern and he looked in visible pain and distress as he was treated by the medical staff before being helped off the pitch.

The fear will be of some kind of ligament damage and Arteta confirmed Copley would be scanned on Thursday. “Quite worried to be fair for the kid because the mechanism doesn’t look very positive and the symptoms that he already has,” the manager said. “So we are going to have to wait and see for tomorrow’s scan. Touch wood that is not a bad one.”

“It is another opportunity for fitness. Coming into games against teams of good quality, it is important we get good fitness. It is a chance to build momentum for the season ahead. We are taking steps in the right direction. Of course, we want more from the performance and the results, but we take learnings and move on.

“All of us, together, we share a common goal. We have confidence to build on. We will take every game as it comes. The atmosphere was amazing. I did not realise Ireland has so many Arsenal fans. We are so grateful for everyone who came to support us today. We wanted to give them a good result and performance. Hopefully next time we can.”

“We always want to win and in the end we could not today. In some ways they deserved it and we could have done better. We did have some good moments though. We had an amazing season last season. I believe in this team and there are still players to come back from the World Cup. I am confident but we can always do better.”

Premier League champions Arsenal continue their preparations for the new season as they take on Spanish side Real Betis at the Aviva Stadium in Dublin, with this being the first-ever meeting between the two sides. This is the first time Arsenal have played a pre-season game in Dublin since August 2018, when they drew 1-1 with London rivals Chelsea, with Arsenal winning on penalties.

Arsenal beat Girona 4-1 in their opening game of pre-season, with new signing Christos Tzolis on the scoresheet in his first appearance for the club. After today, Arsenal have three more games, including the Community Shield, before they kick off the defence of their Premier League crown at home against newly-promoted Coventry on opening night of the new season. They are closing in on a deal for Newcastle captain Bruno Guimaraes, who is set to become one of Arsenal’s most expensive signings. Mikel Arteta’s side have been dealt a blow ahead of the season with the news that France centre-back William Saliba will miss an extended period due to a back injury. The likes of Declan Rice, Bukayo Saka and Noni Madueke are still yet to return after their World Cup exploits.

Real Betis, under the management of former Manchester City boss Manuel Pellegrini, finished fifth in La Liga last season and have therefore qualified for this coming season’s Champions League after a 20-year absence from the competition. Pellegrini has led Betis into European competitions for five consecutive seasons, which includes losing the 2025 Europa Conference League final to Chelsea. They have been busy in pre-season action already having played five games up to this point. They have won four of the five, including a 4-0 victory over Lyon, with just one defeat coming against Granada.

They have a number of former Premier League players in their ranks, including Antony, Giovani Lo Celso, Pablo Fornals and former Arsenal man Hector Bellerin. Real Betis have already played a number of friendlies as they build up to the start of their season, which begins on August 21 when they take on Real Sociedad.

#2491. Rocheteau rend hommage à Pivot

Après le carton de son livre Foot Sentimental paru il y a 3 ans aux éditions Le Cherche midi, Dominique Rocheteau a fait paraître le mois dernier dans la même maison Soif d'Idéal. Dans ce bouquin, l'Ange Vert rend hommage à Bernard Pivot.

"Avant chaque phase finale de Coupe du Monde, Bernard Pivot nous préparait plusieurs valises remplies de livres. Il en dressait la liste qu'il remettait au staff de l'équipe de France. C'était assez varié : classiques, bandes dessinées, polars, science-fiction, et des livres d'histoire que Patrick Battiston, dont je partageais la chambre en Argentine, aimait beaucoup. C'est ainsi que j'ai découvert, en 1978, Lolita de Nabokov, Tendre est la nuit de Scott Fitzgerald. En 1982, lors du Mondial en Espagne, j'ai lu pour la première fois Henry Miller (Big Sur) et Anaïs Nin (Journal). Dans cette petite bibliothèque éphémère, nous pouvions nous servir comme bon nous semblait. J'ai toujours eu du mal à lire les veilles de match, mais sur plusieurs semaines de compétition, nous avions du temps par-ci par-là, en dehors des entraînements. Bernard Pivot, qui était par ailleurs un grand fan de Saint-Etienne, m'a permis de découvrir un certain nombre d'auteurs. Je ne l'en remercierai jamais assez. Il disait d'ailleurs une phrase que je trouve très belle. « Lire, ce n'est pas se retirer du monde, c'est entrer dans le monde par d'autres portes. »

C'est l'idée que je souhaite transmettre aux jeunes lors de mes stages d'été Foot pour Tous. Il m'est arrivé de faire venir des auteurs pour échanger avec les enfants et les adolescents. J'essaie de leur faire comprendre qu'il n'y a pas que le football dans la vie, qu'il est toujours bon de s'intéresser à autre chose, la lecture par exemple, qui a toujours été pour moi une forme d'évasion, de liberté. Elle a été indispensable à mon équilibre dans ma vie d'homme et de footballeur.

Au départ, je n'avais pas une grande culture littéraire, mais plus je grandissais, plus j'aimais lire. Je me rappelle qu'entre 10 et 16 ans, je lisais beaucoup de bandes dessinées, comme Les Passagers du Vent, Blueberry, Prince Vaillant, Les Pionniers du Nouveau Monde, Corto Maltese, Les Aventures de Black et Mortimer, Les Aventures de Tintin ou Gaston Lagaffe.

Les années qui ont suivi Mai 68 - j'avais entre 16 et 20 ans - ont été pour moi une période d'éveil politique. Je m'intéressais à tout ce qui se passait en France et ailleurs. J'étais très sensible au mouvement hippie, au « peace and love ». J'avais cette envie de changer le monde à travers la musique et la lecture. Gandhi m'inspirait beaucoup. C'est à cette époque que j'ai commencé à lire vraiment. En première et en terminale, au lycée du Portail Rouge, à Saint-Étienne, mes amis Jean-Luc, Duche et Jojo étaient très engagés politiquement dans la mouvance anarchiste. Avec eux, mes lectures se sont orientées vers les biographies de Lénine, Trotsky, Bakounine, Che Guevara.

J'ai ensuite commencé à lire les grands écrivains français comme Zola, Hugo, Maupassant, Balzac, qui ont contribué à façonner ma vision du monde. J'aime aussi les auteurs du XXe siècle, comme Proust, Céline, Francis Scott Fitzgerald, Henry Miller... J'ai lu après ma carrière de footballeur de plus en plus de livres de grands auteurs classiques, grâce aux éditions Librio, la collection de poche très facile d'accès. Cela m'a permis de beaucoup lire, tellement j'aimais avoir ces petits livres entre les mains.

Mon livre de chevet reste cependant « Une vie » de Maupassant. Je l'emportais dans ma valise à chacun de mes déplacements. Ce roman m'a profondément touché car il raconte la vie d'une femme, mais aussi la réalité sociale de l'époque. C'est l'histoire de Jeanne, pleine de rêves et d'espoir, qui va finir par connaître de nombreuses désillusions. « La vie, voyez-vous, ce n'est jamais si bon ni si mauvais qu'on croit »...

Mes cinq livres préférés sont Une vie de Guy de Maupassant, Tendre est la nuit de Francis Scott Fitzgerald, L'insoutenable légèreté de l’être de Milan Kundera, Lolita de Vladimir Nabokov et Jours tranquilles à Clichy de Henry Miller.

#2488. Lazio da scudetto, Sensini ci crede: «Ha tutte le qualità»

L’ex biancoceleste elogia il gioco di Gattuso e la forza del gruppo, ma indica nella prima sconfitta una prova da superare.

Secondo quanto riportato da La Gazzetta dello Sport, Nestor Sensini crede nella possibilità che la Lazio vinca lo scudetto. L’ex biancoceleste considera il titolo una sorta di miracolo sportivo, ma vede nella squadra di Gattuso le condizioni per provarci: «Ha tutte le qualità per centrare l’impresa.» Cita l’ambiente, la grinta e la preparazione tattica dell’allenatore, oltre alla consapevolezza che il gruppo sta acquisendo partita dopo partita.

CLICCA QUI E SEGUI LAZIOPRESS.IT ANCHE SU INSTAGRAM

Il legame di Sensini con la Lazio risale al titolo conquistato in biancoceleste. Pur essendo affezionato anche a Udinese e Parma, ricorda soprattutto la festa vissuta a Roma: «Alla Lazio ho conquistato il mio scudetto. Quell’esperienza si è appiccicata alla pelle, la festa di un popolo intero. Come si fa a dimenticare una cosa così?»

Per Sensini, il gioco è uno dei punti di forza della Lazio. Gattuso ha dato alla squadra un’identità precisa: sa difendere e attaccare coinvolgendo l’intero organico. L’ex calciatore attribuisce questo risultato alla capacità del tecnico di convincere tutti della validità delle proprie idee.

Richiamando la sua esperienza in diversi spogliatoi, anche in quelli dove giocava Maradona, Sensini osserva che può esserci qualche scontento perfino quando arrivano i risultati. Alla Lazio, invece, gli sembra di vedere un gruppo unito, senza prime donne né i fuoriclasse della squadra con cui vinse lo scudetto, ma disposto a lavorare per un obiettivo comune. «In questa squadra vedo il carattere del suo allenatore», dice.

Tra i singoli cita Zaccagni, Noslin e Frattesi, pur insistendo sul collettivo e sulla disponibilità a fare una corsa in più per aiutare un compagno in difficoltà.

Sensini riconosce che Inter e Roma hanno rose superiori per qualità e quantità e che i mezzi finanziari della Lazio non sono paragonabili a quelli degli altri club. Per questo fa i complimenti ai tecnici e ai dirigenti che hanno individuato i giocatori adatti al progetto di Gattuso. Ritiene comunque meritata la posizione della Lazio: «Nove vittorie, due pareggi e zero sconfitte: il cammino è fantastico.»

Il suo dubbio riguarda la risposta della squadra a un risultato negativo: «Prima o poi arriverà una sconfitta e bisognerà vedere come il gruppo reagirà alla caduta.» Sensini è convinto che Gattuso saprà affrontare anche quei momenti, purché l’ambiente resti vicino ai giocatori. Sulla corsa al titolo si sbilancia: «Lo stesso dovranno fare i suoi giocatori, e mi sbilancio: alla fine, ce la faranno. E io sarò a festeggiare con loro».

#2485. Cathro fait l'éloge de Csongvai

En conférence de presse hier Ian Cathro, après avoir réfuté toute difficulté de son groupe à performer à l’extérieur, s’est longuement exprimé sur sa gestion de l’effectif, afin de maintenir tout le monde à disposition et est revenu plus précisément sur le cas de son métronome hongrois, Aron Csongvai :

« On se prépare pour un gros match, un match contre un adversaire très fort. Un match qu’on aborde avec beaucoup de motivation, beaucoup d’ambition. Je ne sais pas si le match sera spectaculaire. Je m’en fiche. Gagner me suffit. On aime tous le foot aussi, et c’est beau quand il y a des bons joueurs sur le terrain. Ça sera un gros match, et on est excité à l’idée de jouer un gros match de foot.

Nos difficultés rencontrées parfois à l’extérieur ne sont pas liées à une question d’être à domicile ou à l’extérieur, mais simplement à la façon dont l’adversaire a  su jouer. C’était le cas à Dijon, même si on a gagné, à Dunkerque même si il était très étrange de perdre ce match . Mais les difficultés venaient plus de la façon dont l’adversaire a su nous déséquilibrer que du fait de jouer à l’extérieur. Peut-être que le seul match où le fait de ne pas jouer à domicile a eu un impact était la fin de match à Metz, parce que là l’ambiance a pu jouer.

Contre Rumilly, j’ai préféré notre seconde période à la première. On avait 10 joueurs absents. C’était difficile d’avoir une équipe avec du rythme car les joueurs avaient eu très peu de temps pour trouver des automatismes, peut-être deux entraînements ensemble. La défense a trois a été une option. Je suis très à l’aise avec la défense à trois qui peut être une option bien utile. La seule chose à laquelle je suis marié c’est ma femme. Mon job est d’aider les joueurs à gagner. Une saison de foot est longue. Et ça se joue aussi en fonction des joueurs disponibles. On manage beaucoup le temps de jeu des joueurs pour éviter les problèmes. Donc on doit toujours avoir une option alternative.

Parfois connaître la personne aide à mieux connaître le footballeur. Aron Csongvai fait de l’excellent boulot, il s’est adapté très vite. C’est un mec mature. Il est à une étape de sa vie où il était parfaitement prêt à franchir cette étape, changer de vie, venir dans un plus grand club. N’oublions pas que sa saison a démarré en janvier, ce n’est pas facile. Mais oui il fait vraiment un boulot très important pour l’équipe. Son rôle c’est de garder l’équilibre avec ou sans le ballon. Il a une grosse responsabilité dans le fait que l’équipe reste équilibrée. L’objectif est de le garder en forme sur les 2 ou 3 mois à venir car je le répète il vient d’une compétition qui a démarré en janvier. Quand les autres clôtureront une saison complète, lui il bouclera une saison ½ de compétition. Donc ce que j’attends de lui c’est qu’il parvienne à rester en forme parce qu’il joue un rôle important dans notre organisation. Donc gardons le en bonne santé et essayons quand c’est possible de lui permettre de se reposer un peu.

#2483. Gomes scores for Aston Villa in defeat by Bayern Munich in Hong Kong

Aston Villa’s tour of the Far East ended in defeat as they lost 2-1 to German champions Bayern Munich in a pre-season friendly at the Kai Tak Sports Park in Hong Kong on Friday.

Bayern took the lead inside the final 10 minutes of the first half, with defender Kim Min-jae heading in from Tom Bischof’s sublime free-kick. Villa goalkeeper Marco Bizot made a string of saves in the second half to keep Bayern’s lead at one goal until former Liverpool forward Luis Díaz smashed in Bayern’s second off the underside of the bar.

Just as the game appeared to be out of reach for Aston Villa, who had struggled to create much going forward for most of the game, Unai Emery’s side showed some life late on. Summer signing João Gomes’s goal from the edge of the box gave them hope before Tammy Abraham nearly brought them back onto level terms, only to be denied by a fine save from 40-year-old Manuel Neuer.

Aston Villa are next in action in six days’ time when they take on Paris St-Germain in the Uefa Super Cup before kicking off their Premier League campaign at Brighton. Bayern have one more friendly to come against RB Leipzig before the DFL-Supercup against Borussia Dortmund. They then kick off the defence of their Bundesliga crown against Stuttgart at home on August 28.

Unai Emery’s men head home from the Far East to face PSG in the Uefa Super Cup in Salzburg next week and then have one more pre-season friendly against Borussia Monchengladbach before starting their Premier League campaign at Brighton.

For 80 minutes, Aston Villa barely looked like scoring but come so close to getting two goals in just a few minutes. Abraham meets a cross from the right and must think he has scored but his header is brilliantly saved by the 40-year-old Neuer.

The ball crashes off the underside of the bar and goes in. It has been all Bayern in this second half and that goal had been coming.

It is safe to say the Bayern fans out in Hong Kong love Diaz, who is cheered every time he touches the ball.

Should Bayern have been awarded a penalty there? A ball is played in behind the Villa defence into the penalty area and Ibrahimovic appeared to be brought down but the referee waves those appeals away. On first viewing, that appeared to be a penalty.

“Aston Villa are a top side with a very good coach. They have a large squad and will field plenty of talent. They have already played more friendlies than us, but for us it is a good challenge. It is a match in which we will learn a lot about our team’s fitness. It will be a good match for us.”

“It is difficult to improve; after all, it is not just about the trophies we have won, but also about the number of goals and matches we have won. It is possible, but difficult. Right now, I would say our aim is to win the Champions League; there is no doubt about that. And throughout the season, I will be saying that we need to focus on the next match. To achieve that; to win the Bundesliga, the cup and the Champions League, we need the whole squad to be fit and healthy. We need to make good use of them and improve the squad. We have already done that, but hopefully it will help us achieve our goals.”

Aston Villa’s preparations for the new season continue out in Hong Kong as they take on Bundesliga champions Bayern Munich at the Kai Tak Stadium. These sides have met twice before; Aston Villa beat Bayern 1-0 to win the European Cup in 1981/82 before then meeting in the Champions League group stages during the 2024/25 season, with Jhon Duran’s long-range screamer giving Aston Villa a 1-0 win at Villa Park.

After losing two of their first three pre-season friendlies, Aston Villa have won their first two games on Asian soil and this game marks the end of their tour of the Far East. It has been a summer of significant change at Aston Villa thus far with some key members of their squad departing and some new faces arriving. Youri Tielemans has departed after Manchester United triggered his release clause and they then sold Morgan Rogers to Chelsea for a British transfer record £117m. Unai Emery’s side have brought in 20-year-old Swiss midfielder Johan Manzambi, Brazilian midfielder Joao Gomes and Alejandro Garnacho, with the deal for Garnacho being an initial loan. Manzambi is currently on the sidelines due to a knee injury.

Unai Emery’s side were dealt a blow during the World Cup as Belgian midfielder Amadou Onana picked up a serious knee injury. They were though boosted by the return of Boubacar Kamara after seven months out as he scored in the 3-1 friendly victory over BG Parthum. As one midfielder returned from injury in that game, another picked up a fresh injury as John McGinn suffered a knock to his knee. The likes of Ollie Watkins, Ezri Konsa and Emi Martinez are yet to return after their world Cup exploits. They begin their Premier League campaign with an away game on the south coast against Brighton on August 23 but will be involved in the Uefa Super Cup against PSG next week having lifted the Europa League last season.

Bayern Munich head into this season looking to win a third straight Bundesliga title and have won two of their three friendlies, losing once. They have added Moroccan attacker Ismael Saibari from PSV and German left-back Nathaniel Brown from Bundesliga rivals Eintracht Frankfurt to their ranks this summer. The likes of Harry Kane, Dayot Upamecano and Michael Olise are not available as they are still yet to return after World Cup duty but Vincent Kompany has confirmed that Bayern’s German stars are set to return.

After today, Bayern have one more friendly against RB Leipzig before the DFL-Supercup against Borussia Dortmund. They then kick off the defence of their Bundesliga crown against Stuttgart at home on August 28.

#2474. « Je suis candidat à l'élection présidentielle de 2027 » : L'annonce inattendue de Teddy Riner !

C'est une annonce pour le moins inattendue dans le monde du sport tricolore. Ce lundi 9 novembre 2026, Teddy Riner a officialisé sa candidature à l'élection présidentielle de 2027 dans une vidéo publiée sur son compte X, tout en lançant son propre mouvement politique intitulé « La France qu'on veut ».

Le judoka français de 37 ans se lance ainsi en politique après avoir dominé le judo international. Quintuple champion olympique et onze fois champion du monde, Teddy Riner a bâti un palmarès exceptionnel qui le place au sommet de sa discipline. Alors que son avenir sportif restait au cœur des discussions depuis les Jeux de Paris, le Français surprend en ouvrant un tout autre chapitre personnel. Un virage spectaculaire.

Dans une vidéo publiée ce lundi 9 novembre 2026 sur son compte X, Teddy Riner a pris la parole face caméra afin de lever le voile sur ses intentions pour la prochaine présidentielle. Le judoka de 37 ans a officialisé sa démarche avec des mots simples et directs.

Conscient des réactions que son entrée en politique peut susciter, le quintuple champion olympique a tenu à devancer immédiatement les critiques concernant son manque d'expérience dans les fonctions publiques. Teddy Riner a ainsi déclaré : « Je sais ce qu'on va dire de moi. Il est fou, il n'est pas du sérail, il n'a pas les codes, il n'a jamais fait de politique. Mais posez-vous la question avec moi, qu'est-ce qui est fou aujourd'hui ? Je n'ai pas passé ma vie derrière un public, mais le combat a toujours fait partie de moi. » Une détermination affichée.

Pour concrétiser ses ambitions, le judoka français de 37 ans a également fondé sa propre formation politique ce lundi 9 novembre 2026, baptisée « La France qu'on veut ». Cette prise de parole sur les réseaux sociaux a d'ailleurs été accompagnée d'un entretien accordé au quotidien Le Parisien pour préciser le sens de sa démarche. Dans cet échange, Teddy Riner revendique une volonté de rassemblement sans affiliation partisane, affirmant vouloir devenir un « président qui fédère » sans se situer à droite ou à gauche.

En effet, le champion tricolore a expliqué que sa décision avait mûri dans le sillage direct de l'élan collectif suscité par les Jeux olympiques de Paris 2024. Porté par cette dynamique populaire, il entend désormais transposer cette ferveur sur le plan politique. Ainsi, son mouvement politique refuse d'entrer dans les clivages traditionnels pour privilégier une adhésion commune à l'échelle du pays. Un projet ambitieux.

Toutefois, cette ambition politique ne pousse pas Teddy Riner vers la retraite sportive. Sur le plan des compétitions, sa dernière apparition individuelle remonte aux Jeux olympiques de Paris en août 2024, suivie d'une participation sous les couleurs du PSG Judo en Ligue des champions en décembre 2024. Malgré cette nouvelle démarche politique, le quintuple champion olympique et onze fois champion du monde de judo demeure bel et bien un athlète en activité.

Au-delà de l'élection présidentielle de 2027, Teddy Riner a ainsi fait part de son souhait de participer aux Jeux olympiques de Los Angeles 2028 si ses futures responsabilités et les Français le lui permettent. Le judoka français lie donc son avenir sportif à la suite de son engagement public, avec en ligne de mire les Jeux olympiques de Los Angeles 2028.

#2473. Mamadou Coulibaly (AS Monaco) s'excuse après son geste polémique

Auteur de son premier but dans l'élite lors du match nul sur le terrain sarthois, Mamadou Coulibaly a suscité une vive polémique en mimant un geste d'égorgement. Le jeune milieu de l'AS Monaco se retrouve désormais sous la menace directe d'une lourde suspension.

Dimanche 8 novembre 2026, l'AS Monaco a été accrochée sur la pelouse du Mans FC (1-1) pour le compte de la 10e journée de Ligue 1. Si le club du Rocher espérait engranger des points précieux, la rencontre a été marquée par le geste de son joueur de 22 ans.

Titularisé dans l'entrejeu monégasque, Mamadou Coulibaly pensait passer une bonne soirée après avoir ouvert le score à la 40e minute de jeu. Mais la joie d'inscrire son tout premier but en Ligue 1 a rapidement viré au malaise lors de sa célébration. Le milieu monégasque s'est en effet dirigé vers le poteau de corner pour glisser sur les genoux, avant de tirer la langue et de mimer un geste d'égorgement avec son pouce sous la gorge. Un geste polémique.

Ce geste n'est malheureusement pas inédit en Principauté cette saison. En effet, cette célébration reproduit à l'identique celle effectuée par son partenaire de club Paris Brunner face à Strasbourg en septembre dernier, lors de la 4e journée de Ligue 1. Pour ce geste, le jeune attaquant avait été sanctionné par la commission de discipline de la LFP de quatre matches de suspension, dont deux fermes et deux avec sursis.

D'ailleurs, cette récidive au sein du même effectif met le joueur dans une situation extrêmement délicate. En raison de ce précédent disciplinaire très récent, Mamadou Coulibaly risque une sanction similaire d'au moins deux matches de suspension ferme devant la commission de discipline de la LFP. L'instance disciplinaire devrait se saisir rapidement du dossier pour trancher sur ce comportement. Un coup dur en perspective.

Conscient des remous provoqués par son geste, le joueur de 22 ans s'est exprimé devant les journalistes à l'issue de la rencontre. « Je tiens à m’excuser parce que j’ai cru comprendre que ça avait été mal compris. C’est un geste que j’ai fait par rapport un ami à moi qui m’a conseillé au sujet de la célébration. Je tiens à m'excuser si ça a pu heurter des gens. Maintenant, ce sont des choses qui se font dans d'autres championnats, je n'ai pas pensé que ça pourrait avoir des conséquences négatives », a ainsi déclaré Mamadou Coulibaly en zone mixte au micro des médias après Le Mans-Monaco.

Toutefois, cette justification risque de ne pas suffire auprès de son entraîneur Filipe Luis. Le coach monégasque avait pourtant publiquement averti son groupe en septembre après l'affaire similaire, insistant sur l'exemplarité attendue : « Il a fait une erreur. Il l'a compris tout de suite. Il s'est excusé. Le club prend cette affaire très au sérieux (…). On espère que ça ne se reproduira plus », avait prévenu le technicien en conférence de presse. Au-delà de cette polémique extra-sportive, l'AS Monaco accueillera l'AJ Auxerre au stade Louis-II le samedi 21 novembre 2026 à 20h45 pour la 11e journée de Ligue 1.
